Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in stellar theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 909 and the invariant I(A,B) = 38 mod 4.

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^38(S, F) is trivial. QED.
